## Ontario Fishery Regulations, 2007 {#ontario-fishery-regulations-2007 .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2007-237
:::

[FISHERIES ACT](/eng/acts/F-14)

Registration 2007-10-25

Ontario Fishery Regulations, 2007

P.C. 2007-1648 2007-10-25
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Fisheries and Oceans, pursuant to section 43[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_609023-E_hq_2968){#fn_609023-E_hq_2968-ID0EFBAA}
of the [Fisheries Act](/eng/acts/F-14), hereby makes the annexed
[Ontario Fishery Regulations, 2007](/eng/regulations/SOR-2007-237).

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_609023-E_hq_2968-ID0EFBAA){#fn_609023-E_hq_2968}S.C.
  1991, c. 1, s. 12
  :::
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-735463 .Part}

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[angling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[angling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means fishing with a line that is
      held in the hand or attached to a rod that is held in the hand or
      closely attended. ([à la ligne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[artificial fly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[artificial fly]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hook dressed with
      silk, wool, fur, feathers or other similar organic or non-organic
      material that is used to catch fish when angling and includes wet
      flies, dry flies and streamer flies but does not include an
      artificial lure or organic bait. ([mouche
      artificielle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[artificial lure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[artificial lure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a spoon, plug, jig
      or other device that is designed to catch fish when
      angling. ([leurre artificiel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[baitfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[baitfish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fish of a species native
      to Ontario that is set out in Part 2 of Schedule
      1. ([poisson-appât]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[barbless hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[barbless hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a hook without barbs
      or a hook that has barbs that are compressed so as to be in
      complete contact with the shaft of the hook. ([hameçon sans
      ardillon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[border waters area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[border waters area]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2017-195, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[fisheries management zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fisheries management zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} or
      [[zone]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a subdivision of the waters of
      Ontario as shown in the Regulation Plans of Fisheries Management
      Zones filed on December 5, 2013 in the Office of the Surveyor
      General of Ontario. ([zone de gestion des pêches]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"} ou [zone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a single or multiple-pointed
      hook on a common shaft but does not include a
      snagger. ([hameçon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[invasive fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[invasive fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2015-121, s.
      37\]]{.Repealed}

  [[invasive species]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[invasive species]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a species that is
      set out, in respect of Ontario, in Part 2 of the schedule to the
      [Aquatic Invasive Species
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-121). ([espèce
      envahissante]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[licence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a licence or permit issued under the Fish and
        Wildlife Conservation Act, 1997, Statutes of Ontario 1997, c. 41
        or a document or thing deemed to be a licence under that Act;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a licence issued under the [Aboriginal Communal
        Fishing Licences Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332); or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a licence issued under these
        Regulations. ([permis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[livewell]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[livewell]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a compartment designed to
      keep fish alive that is attached to or forms part of a fishing
      vessel, has the capacity for water exchange and aeration and
      contains no less than 46 L of water. ([vivier]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[plant-based bait]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[plant-based bait]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means either

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} bait that is made exclusively from plants; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} bait that is made primarily from plants if the
        following conditions are met:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} it does not contain visible pieces of fish,
          animal parts or animal ingredients;

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} it does not have natural or artificial meat
          or fish flavouring; and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any poultry eggs that are incorporated are
          used as a binding agent. ([appât d'origine
          végétale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[pound net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[pound net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an impounding net whose
      top is not covered by netting. ([parc en filet]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[provincial Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Minister of
      Natural Resources and Forestry for Ontario. ([ministre
      provincial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[resident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person whose residence is
      in Canada and who has resided in Canada for a period of at least 6
      months during the 12 months immediately before the day on which
      that residence becomes material for the purposes of these
      Regulations. ([résident]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[seine net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[seine net]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a net, the ends of which
      are brought together or hauled ashore, and includes a bar seine,
      beach seine, drag seine, purse seine or other similar type of
      net. ([senne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[snagger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[snagger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an instrument that is
      designed for the purpose of hooking or piercing a fish in any part
      of the body other than the mouth. ([croc]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[spear gun]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[spear gun]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any device, other than
      a bow, that is capable of propelling a spear. ([fusil à
      harpon]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[specially protected fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[specially protected fish]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fish of a
      species set out in Part 4 of Schedule 1. ([spécialement
      protégé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sport fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sport fishing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the catching of fish
      for non-commercial purposes. ([pêche sportive]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[spring gaff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[spring gaff]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a spring-operated device
      for impaling or hooking a fish and includes spring-loaded hooks
      that are triggered when a fish bites the bait. ([gaffe à
      ressort]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[stringer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[stringer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a string or metal line on
      which fish are strung. ([chaîne ou corde à
      poissons]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[trolling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trolling]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means angling by trailing lures
      or bait behind a fishing vessel that is in constant forward
      motion. ([pêche à la traine]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of determining the size of a fish, a
  fish shall be measured from the tip of the head with the jaws closed
  to the tip of the tail with the lobes compressed so as to give the
  maximum possible measurement.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A reference in these Regulations to a species or
  group of species of fish by its common name as set out in column 1 of
  Part 1, 2 or 4 of Schedule 1 is a reference to the species or group of
  species of fish whose scientific name is set out in column 2.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-735496 .Part}

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} These
  Regulations apply in Ontario.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} waters to which the [National Parks of Canada
    Fishing Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1120) apply;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} activities carried out under an aquaculture
    licence;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} activities carried out, in the course of their
    duties, by employees of the Ontario Ministry of Natural Resources
    and Forestry and employees of the Department of Fisheries and
    Oceans; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} artificially created waters into which only fish
    obtained from either the holder of an aquaculture licence or the
    holder of a commercial fishing licence have been released for
    non-commercial purposes if those waters

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} are not on a regional flood plain,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} lie wholly within the boundaries of privately
      owned land,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} have no connection or outflow to natural
      waters, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} contain water from surface run-off, natural
      springs, ground water or water pumped from a stream or lake.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations, other than subsections 1(1) and
  (3), paragraphs 3(1)(b) and (c), subsection 4(3) to (6) and sections
  13 and 44, do not apply to fishing and related activities carried out
  under a licence issued under the [Aboriginal Communal Fishing Licences
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-93-332).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 3
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 6](#1428753-1437600){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428753-1437600.html"
  role="button"}
:::

:::

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-735511 .Part}

### [Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735512 .Subheading}

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall, except as authorized under a licence,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ship or transport or attempt to ship or transport
    live fish other than baitfish; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} deposit or attempt to deposit live fish into any
    body of water other than the body of water from which they were
    caught.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2023-245, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 4
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 7](#1428755-1437695){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428755-1437695.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-3-20181023.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Terms and Conditions of Licences]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735521 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2011-154, s. 1(E)

\]
:::

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  provincial Minister may, for the proper management and control of
  fisheries and the conservation and protection of fish, specify in a
  licence any term or condition that is not inconsistent with these
  Regulations respecting one or more of the following matters:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the species, quantity, size, weight, age, sex or
    stage of development of fish that may be caught, retained,
    possessed, held, loaded, landed, transported, transferred or
    released;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the waters or locations where fishing is permitted
    or from which fish may be caught;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the persons who may carry out activities under the
    licence;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the waters or locations where fish may be held,
    loaded, landed, transferred or released;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the period during which fishing is permitted or
    fish may be transferred or released;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the type, size, quantity or marking of fishing gear
    or equipment;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the locations or manner in which fishing gear or
    equipment may be used;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the fishing vessel that may be used and the persons
    who may operate it;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the weighing, marking, tagging, loading, landing,
    handling, separation or transportation of fish;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the type and marking of containers used to hold or
    transport fish;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the information that shall be recorded, kept or
    reported and the manner and form in which and the period for which
    the information shall be recorded, kept or reported;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} the disposing of water, containers or anything used
    to hold or transport fish;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} the monitoring of fishing activities, verification
    or examination of fishing gear, equipment, fish or records relating
    to fishing activities and the taking of fish samples;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} the health of fish including the monitoring and
    reporting of water quality, diseases, pathogens or fish escapes;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} the possession, destruction or prevention of the
    spread of a fish or other aquatic organism that is or may be harmful
    to fish;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} the method of movement, transfer, release, disposal
    or preservation of fish to ensure their protection; or

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} the information that the master of a fishing vessel
    shall report from the water, including the method by which, the
    period in which and the person to whom the report is to be made.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person shall comply with the terms and
  conditions specified in their licence.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The provincial Minister may amend the terms and
  conditions of a licence for the purposes of the conservation and
  protection of fish.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A notice of an amendment made under subsection (3)
  shall be sent to the licence holder. The notice is deemed to be
  received by a licence holder

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the licence holder consents to receiving it by
    electronic means, on the day recorded by the server of the
    provincial Minister as the time of sending it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the licence holder consents to receiving it by
    fax, on the day recorded by a fax machine of the provincial Minister
    as the time of sending it;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if it is sent to the licence holder by mail, on the
    fifth day after the postmark date on the notice; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if it is delivered personally, on the day on which
    the notice is received by the licence holder.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} The amended licence terms take effect on the day on
  which the notice is received by the licence holder.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} The licence holder shall, on receipt of the notice,
  attach the notice to the licence.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 5
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Prohibition on Bringing Bait Into Ontario]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735554 .Subheading}

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** No person shall bring into
Ontario, for use as bait,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} crayfish or salamanders; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} live fish or leeches.

### [Invasive Species]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735558 .Subheading}

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess, transport or release members of an invasive
  species except in accordance with the [Aquatic Invasive Species
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-121) or as authorized under a
  licence issued under subsection (2).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The provincial Minister may issue a licence to a
  person to possess, transport or release invasive species in specified
  quantities if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are an employee or a person referred to in
    subsection 13(1) of the [Aquatic Invasive Species
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2015-121);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} they use the equipment and controls necessary to
    protect against any unauthorized release of invasive species into
    Ontario waters; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the possession is for scientific, educational or
    aquatic invasive species control purposes.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-121, s. 38
- SOR/2018-216, s. 6
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20150529.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Specially Protected Fish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735567 .Subheading}

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish for or possess a specially protected fish except as
  authorized under a licence issued under subsection (2).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The provincial Minister may issue a licence to a
  person to fish for or possess specially protected fish if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} they are a qualified fisheries researcher working
    in a recognized research facility; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the purpose of their fishing or possession is the
    conservation or protection of the species.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a person may possess an
  American eel if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an eel that is 10 cm or more in
    length, the person has documentary evidence to show that the eel was
    lawfully obtained outside of Ontario; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an eel that is less than 10 cm in
    length, the possession is authorized under the [Possession and
    Export of Elvers Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2024-237).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 7
- [SOR/2024-237, s. 16](#1496705-1516419){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1496705-1516419.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20181023.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Close Times Applicable to Fish Sanctuaries]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735576 .Subheading}

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish in the waters set out in column I of an item of
  Schedule V to the [Ontario Fishery Regulations,
  1989](/eng/regulations/SOR-89-93), during the close time set out in
  column II of that item.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2009-328, s. 2\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-328, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Prohibited Fishing Methods and Gear]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735582 .Subheading}

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall fish with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a hook used in such a manner as to pierce or hook a
    fish in any part of the body other than the mouth;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a gaff;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a snare;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a snagger; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a spear gun.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(b), a person may use a gaff,
  other than a spring gaff, to assist in the landing of a fish.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall retain a fish that has been pierced
  or hooked in any part of the body other than the mouth while angling
  except a fish that has been pierced or hooked while being landed with
  a gaff that is not a spring gaff.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-311, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess a snagger, spear gun or spring gaff in any waters
  or within 30 m of any water's edge.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except when spear fishing in accordance with these
  Regulations, no person shall possess a spear for the purpose of
  fishing in any waters or within 30 m of any water's edge.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess a snare for the purpose of
  fishing in any waters or within 30 m of any water's edge.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 8
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Use of Artificial Light]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735600 .Subheading}

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** No person shall use an
artificial light to attract fish except

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to fish for lake herring, lake whitefish or smelt by
  any means other than by angling; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} when angling, as part of a lure attached to a line.

### [Release of Fish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735604 .Subheading}

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** A person, other than a
person fishing under a commercial fishing licence, who catches a fish,
other than an invasive species, the retention or possession of which is
prohibited by these Regulations, shall immediately return the fish to
the waters from which it was caught and, if the fish is alive, release
it in a manner that causes the least harm to that fish.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2015-121, s. 39
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Variation Orders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735608 .Subheading}

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  close time, fishing quota or limit on the size or weight of fish or
  fishing gear or equipment has been fixed in respect of an area, the
  provincial Minister may, by order, vary that close time, fishing quota
  or limit on fish or fishing gear or equipment in respect of the area
  or any portion of the area.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a close time, fishing quota, limit on the size or
  weight of fish or fishing gear or equipment is varied, the provincial
  Minister shall give notice of the variation to the persons affected or
  likely to be affected by the variation by one or more of the following
  methods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} broadcasting the notice over a radio station;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} publishing the notice in a newspaper;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} posting the notice in the area or in the vicinity
    of the area affected by the variation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} giving written or oral notice;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} transmitting or posting the notice by electronic
    means; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} publishing the notice in the Ontario Ministry of
    Natural Resources and Forestry summary of regulations pertaining to
    sport fishing.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 9
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 8](#1428756-1437599){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428756-1437599.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Sport Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-735620 .Part}

### [Sport Fishing Methods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735621 .Subheading}

**[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** No person shall sport
fish other than by angling or by means of a spear, dip net, baitfish
trap, seine net, or bow and arrow.

### [Angling Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735623 .Subheading}

**[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** No person shall angle
for, or catch by angling and retain, fish of a species set out in column
1 of Schedule 2 in the waters set out in column 2 during the close time
set out in column 3.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 10(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Angling Quotas and Size Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735627 .Subheading}

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 19 to
21, no person who is fishing under a sport fishing licence or a
conservation fishing licence shall catch and retain in any one day, or
possess, fish of a species set out in column 2 of Part 1 of Schedule 3
from the waters set out in column 1,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in an amount that exceeds the sport fishing licence
  quota or of a size that does not comply with the size limit set out in
  column 3; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in an amount that exceeds the conservation fishing
  licence quota or of a size that does not comply with the size limit
  set out in column 4.

### [Provincial Possession Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735631 .Subheading}

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 18, no
person shall possess fish of a species set out in column 1 of Part 2 of
Schedule 3, caught under a sport fishing licence or conservation fishing
licence from the waters of all zones combined, in a quantity greater
than the provincial possession limit set out in column 2.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-311, s. 3
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-17-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Provincial trout and salmon Aggregate Quota]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735635 .Subheading}

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain in any one day, or possess, a quantity of trout or salmon from
the waters of all zones combined that in the aggregate is greater than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} five, when fishing under a sport fishing licence; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} two, when fishing under a conservation fishing
  licence.

### [Daily Catch and Retain Quotas and Size Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735639 .Subheading}

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** No person shall catch and
retain in any one day fish of a species set out in column 3 of Part 3 of
Schedule 3 as specified for the type of licence set out in column 1 from
the waters set out in column 2 in an amount that exceeds the quota or
does not comply with the size limit set out in column 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 11(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Non-resident Daily Catch and Retain Quotas and Size Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735643 .Subheading}

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** No non-resident shall
catch and retain in any one day fish of a species set out in column 3 of
Part 4 of Schedule 3 as specified for the type of licence set out in
column 1 from the waters set out in column 2 in an amount that exceeds
the quota or of a size that does not comply with the size limit set out
in column 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2017-195, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-20-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 20"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Non-resident Quotas and Size Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735647 .Subheading}

**[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** No non-resident shall
catch and retain in any one day or possess fish of a species set out in
column 3 of Part 5 of Schedule 3 as specified for the type of licence
set out in column 1 from the waters set out in column 2 in an amount
that exceeds the quota or of a size that does not comply with the size
limit set out in column 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 12
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-21-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 21"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** If a non-resident under
18 years of age fishes under a sport fishing licence held by another
person, the fish caught and retained or possessed shall be deemed to be
caught and retained or possessed by the licence holder.

### [Angling From a Fishing Vessel]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735652 .Subheading}

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who is angling under a sport fishing licence or a conservation
  fishing licence from a fishing vessel and who catches largemouth bass,
  smallmouth bass, walleye or northern pike may exchange one or more of
  the live fish that the person has caught for another of the same
  species if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the live fish that are caught and not immediately
    released are held in a livewell that is mechanically aerated at all
    times; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the fish that is being released will survive after
    release and is released into the waters from which it was caught.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person who is fishing under a sport fishing licence
  may hold in the livewell no more than an aggregate of six largemouth
  bass or smallmouth bass.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 13(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Sport Fishing Quotas in Boundary Waters]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735660 .Subheading}

**[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** If a person is sport
fishing in waters that lie both in Ontario and in another jurisdiction,
the fish caught anywhere in the waters shall be deemed to have been
caught and retained or possessed by the person in Ontario for the
purposes of these Regulations.

### [Possession of Fish Caught By Angling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735662 .Subheading}

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall possess a live brook trout or live lake trout caught by
  angling from Lake Nipigon and its tributaries.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), paragraph
  29(2)(d), section 38, paragraph 42(2)(b) and Schedule 4, a reference
  to Lake Nipigon and its tributaries means Lake Nipigon and its
  tributaries downstream from the first falls, rapids, dam or lake ---
  or the entire tributary if there are no falls, rapids, dam or lake ---
  including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the waters on the islands of Lake Nipigon;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Gull River, downstream from the
    Highway 527 bridge;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Kabitotikwia River, downstream
    from the Highway 527 bridge;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Poshkokagan River, downstream
    from and including the rapids located at 49°25′45″ N, 89°04′55″ W;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Wabinosh River, downstream from
    Wabinosh Lake; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the waters of the Little Jackfish River, downstream
    from and including the first rapids upstream of the Pikitigushi Road
    bridge.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 14
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 9](#1428761-1437601){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428761-1437601.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-25-20181023.html "Link to previous version of section 25"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** No person shall possess a
live fish, other than a baitfish, caught by angling from the following
waters in Zone 4:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Lac Seul (50°20′03″ N, 92°45′00″ W);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Broad Lake (50°26′32″ N, 92°37′50″ W);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} Sunlight Lake (50°29′14″ N, 92°34′17″ W) and all
  connecting waters;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} Abram Lake (50°03′33″ N, 91°55′44″ W);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} Botsford Lake (50°08′19″ N, 91°39′32″ W);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} Hidden Lake (50°07′26″ N, 91°44′33″ W);

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} Minnitaki Lake (49°58′19″ N, 91°57′29″ W);

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} Pelican Lake (50°06′41″ N, 91°57′40″ W) and all
  connecting waters, including the waters locally known as Duck Lake;
  and

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the waters collectively known as the Rice River,
  including Flower Lake (49°56′47″ N, 91°57′19″ W), Parnes Lake
  (49°56′27″ N, 91°54′23″ W), Twill Lake (49°56′38″ N, 91°59′02″ W),
  Twin Bay of Minnitaki Lake (49°57′48″ N, 91°52′56″ W), Twinflower Lake
  (49°56′27″ N, 91°55′44″ W), Twinflower Creek and all connecting
  streams.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 15(E)
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 10](#1428763-1438573){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428763-1438573.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-26-20181023.html "Link to previous version of section 26"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  non-resident who is angling from a fishing vessel shall possess on
  board the vessel more fish of a species than the quota set out in
  section 20 for the species in the waters in which the person is
  angling.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} This section applies to the following waters in
  Zone 5:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Lake of the Woods (49°16′59″ N, 94°34′15″ W);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} Shoal Lake (49°32′50″ N, 94°56′25″ W);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} Cul de Sac Lake (49°37′54″ N, 94°49′47″ W);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} Obabikon Lake (49°14′59″ N, 94°12′25″ W);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} Rainy River; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} Rainy Lake (48°43′35″ N, 93°07′51″ W) and the Seine
    River system upstream to the Sturgeon Falls (Crilly) Dam, situated
    immediately north of Highway 11, including the waters of Grassy Lake
    (48°39′53″ N, 92°43′05″ W), Wild Potato Lake (48°43′00″ N,
    92°28′36″ W) and Partridge Crop Lake (48°43′46″ N, 92°22′12″ W) in
    the Territorial Districts of Kenora and Rainy River.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 11](#1428765-1438685){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428765-1438685.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-27-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 27"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Possession or Use of Bait]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735674 .Subheading}

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall release or deposit, or attempt to release or deposit,
  into any waters, or within 30 m of any waters, any of the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} live or dead bait or baitfish, including fish eggs,
    gametes or parts; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the water, soil or other materials in the container
    used to hold the materials set out in paragraph (a).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of
  plant-based bait.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 1(b) does not apply to water that is taken
  from the waters where the person is fishing, used in either a
  recirculating device attached to or forming part of a fishing vessel
  or a moveable container holding bait or baitfish, and deposited back
  into the same waters or within 30 m of those waters.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 16(F)
- [SOR/2020-276, s. 2](#1278767-1279098){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1278767-1279098.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28-20181023.html "Link to previous version of section 28"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[28.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.1 .sectionLabel}** No person, other than
the holder of a commercial bait licence or any other licence that
authorizes the culture of baitfish, shall catch and retain in any one
day, or possess, more than 120 baitfish.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-328, s. 3
- SOR/2014-311, s. 4
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-28.1-20091210.html "Link to previous version of section 28.1"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall use as bait, or possess for use as bait, an invasive
  species or a live fish that is not a species of baitfish.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall use as bait or possess for use as
  bait

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a live baitfish in the waters set out in column 1
    of Part 1 of Schedule 5;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a fish greater than 13 cm in length in Lake
    Temagami (46°59′36″ N, 80°04′15″ W);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a fish

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the waters of Clearwater Bay of Lake of the
      Woods (49°42′23″ N, 94°45′13″ W), Echo Bay of Lake of the Woods
      (49°39′00″ N, 94°53′58″ W) or Cul de Sac Lake (49°37′54″ N,
      94°49′47″ W);

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the waters set out in column 1 of Part 2 of
      Schedule 5 during the period set out in column 2; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} rainbow smelt in Zone 2, 4, 5 or 6, except, in
    Zones 2 and 6, in the waters of Lake Nipigon and its tributaries
    and, in Zone 6, in the waters of Jessie Lake (49°11′19″ N, 88°19′52″
    W), Helen Lake (49°04′20″ N, 88°16′17″ W), Polly Lake (49°07′52″ N,
    88°15′58″ W) and the Nipigon River.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a person may possess no more
  than 36 live crayfish for use as bait in the waters in which the
  person is angling, if the crayfish were caught in those waters.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} No person shall transport crayfish overland except
  under a licence to collect fish for scientific purposes issued under
  the Fish and Wildlife Conservation Act, 1997, Statutes of Ontario,
  1997, c. 41.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (4), a person may transport red
  swamp crayfish ([Procambarus clarkii]{.otherLang lang="la"}) in
  accordance with General, Ontario Regulation 354/16 made under the
  Invasive Species Act, 2015, S.O. 2015, c. 22, as amended from time to
  time.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2008-99, s. 14
- SOR/2014-311, s. 5
- SOR/2015-121, s. 40
- SOR/2018-216, s. 17
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 12](#1428767-1437674){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428767-1437674.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29-20181023.html "Link to previous version of section 29"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Angling Restrictions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735694 .Subheading}

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** No person shall angle
within 25 m of a pound net or cage in which fish are held for culture.

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** No person shall angle in
the waters set out in column 1 of Part 1 of Schedule 4 except in
accordance with the limits on gear and equipment set out in column 2
during the specified time periods, as applicable.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-328, s. 4
- SOR/2014-311, s. 6
- SOR/2018-216, s. 18
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 13](#1428776-1437696){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428776-1437696.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20181023.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no person
  angling in open water shall angle in the waters set out in column 1 of
  Part 2 of Schedule 4 with more than the number of lines set out in
  column 2.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), no person angling through
  ice shall angle in the waters set out in column 1 of Part 3 of
  Schedule 4 with more than the number of lines set out in column 2.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who is angling for common carp may use up to
  three lines in the waters of Zones 12 to 20 if all of the following
  conditions are met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person is using plant-based bait or synthetic
    corn;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when the person is angling from a fishing vessel,
    all of the lines used by the person are on that vessel with the
    person; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} when the person is not angling from a fishing
    vessel, each line used by the person is within 2 m of another line
    used by the person.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2020-276, s. 3](#1278769-1279105){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1278769-1279105.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2023-245, s. 13](#1428776-1437697){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1428776-1437697.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-32-20201216.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** No person who is angling
through the ice shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} be more than 60 m from a hole in the ice through
  which they are angling; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} fail to maintain a clear and unobstructed view of a
  line with which they are angling.

### [Fishing at Night]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735728 .Subheading}

- **[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall sport fish during the period between sunset and sunrise.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a person who is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} angling, except in the waters of Eagle Lake
    (49º42′ N, 93º13′ W);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} fishing by means of a baitfish trap;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fishing for rainbow smelt by means of a dip net or
    seine net; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} fishing for lake herring or lake whitefish by means
    of a dip net.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 19(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Sport Fishing Other Than Angling]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735738 .Subheading}

- **[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person shall sport fish using

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a dip net

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} with dimensions that are greater than 183 cm by
      183 cm if the dip net is angular, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} with a diameter that is greater than 183 cm if
      the dip net is circular;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a seine net with dimensions that are greater than
    10 m by 2 m;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a baitfish trap of a length that is greater than
    51 cm or a diameter that is greater than 31 cm;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} more than one dip net, baitfish trap or seine net;
    or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a baitfish trap unless it is legibly marked with
    their name and address.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person who is sport fishing by a means other than
  by angling shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} catch and retain in any one day or possess fish of
    a species set out in column 1 of Part 1 of Schedule 6 from the
    waters set out in column 3 in an amount that exceeds the quota set
    out in column 5 or 6, as the case may be;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} use any gear other than the gear set out in column
    2 of Part 1 of Schedule 6 for fishing for a species set out in
    column 1;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fish for, or catch and retain, a species of fish
    set out in column 1 of Part 1 of Schedule 6 from the waters set out
    in column 3 during the close time set out in column 4;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} fish for, or catch and retain, a species of fish
    set out in column 1 of Part 1 of Schedule 6 from any waters other
    than the waters set out in column 3; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} fish for, or catch and retain, any species of fish
    other than the species set out in column 1 of Parts 1 and 2 of
    Schedule 6.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2011-154, s. 2
- SOR/2018-216, s. 20(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-35-20110729.html "Link to previous version of section 35"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who is sport fishing by a means other than angling shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 28.1, catch and retain in any
    one day or possess more baitfish than the quota set out in column 5
    of Part 2 of Schedule 6;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} when fishing for baitfish, use any gear other than
    the gear set out in column 2 of Part 2 of Schedule 6; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} fish for baitfish during the close time set out in
    column 4 of Part 2 of Schedule 6.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Other than suckers or lake herring, no non-resident
  shall fish for, or catch and retain baitfish, by a means other than by
  angling.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-328, s. 5
- SOR/2018-216, s. 21
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-36-20091210.html "Link to previous version of section 36"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Live Holding Methods]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735764 .Subheading}

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who is sport fishing shall place or use in any waters a live
  holding box or impounding device unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is legibly marked with the licence holder's name
    and address; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the markings are visible without raising it from
    the water.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a live holding box that forms part of or is
    attached to a fishing vessel; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a bucket or other moveable container that contains
    baitfish.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 22(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-37-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 37"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who is sport fishing shall use a stringer, impounding device or
  live holding box for holding fish in Lake Nipigon and its tributaries.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a person who is sport fishing
  may hold fish, other than brook trout or lake trout, in a livewell on
  Lake Nipigon or its tributaries.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 23
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 38"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Identification and Measurement of Fish]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735779 .Subheading}

- **[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  the fish is being prepared for immediate consumption, no person shall
  skin, cut or pack any fish caught by sport fishing in such a manner
  that the species cannot easily be identified or the number cannot
  easily be counted.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person shall possess a fish that is skinned, cut
  or packed in contravention of subsection (1).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who is in possession of a fish caught by
  sport fishing for which there is an applicable size limit shall keep
  the fish in a manner that allows the size to be easily measured,
  unless the fish

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is being prepared for immediate consumption;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is prepared at an overnight accommodation for
    storage;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} is being transported by the person on the water
    from a temporary overnight accommodation and that person is not
    sport fishing; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} is being transported overland.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (3) does not apply to a person who
  possesses

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} fish from which samples have been collected for
    scientific purposes by an employee authorized by the provincial
    Minister or a person who possesses fish under a licence to collect
    fish for scientific purposes issued under the Fish and Wildlife
    Conservation Act, 1997, Statutes of Ontario, 1997, c. 41; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} documentation given by the person or employee
    containing information on the samples and their collection including
    the original length or weight of the fish.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 24
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-39-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 39"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Commercial Fishing and Commercial Bait Fishing]{.HTitleText1} {#h-735793 .Part}

### [Commercial Baitfish Gear]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735794 .Subheading}

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** No person who is fishing
for baitfish under a commercial bait licence shall use a dip net

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} with dimensions that are greater than 305 cm by
  305 cm if the dip net is angular; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} with a diameter that is greater than 305 cm if the
  dip net is circular.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-311, s. 7
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-40-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 40"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Gear Markings]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735800 .Subheading}

- **[[41]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-41 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who is fishing under a commercial bait licence shall place or
  use a baitfish trap that is not legibly marked with the licence
  holder's name or licence number.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} No person who is operating under a commercial fishing
  licence or a commercial bait licence shall place or use in any waters
  a live holding box or impounding device unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is legibly marked with the licence holder's name
    or licence number, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the markings are visible without raising the box or
    device from the water.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2009-328, s. 6
- SOR/2014-311, s. 8
- SOR/2018-216, s. 25(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-41-20141212.html "Link to previous version of section 41"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[42]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-42 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  person who is fishing under a commercial fishing licence shall set or
  use a hookline or net unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a buoy, marker or stake is attached to the hookline
    or to each end of the net;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} each buoy, marker or stake is legibly marked with
    the licence holder's name or licence number; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the licence holder's name or licence number is
    visible without raising the gear from the water.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(a), and except in the case of
  ice fishing,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the waters of Lake of the Woods and Shoal Lake,
    the hookline or each end of the net must be marked by a buoy with a
    shaft whose upper extremity is at least 53 cm above the water and
    has attached to it an orange flag measuring at least 22 cm by 22 cm;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the waters of Lake Superior and Lake Nipigon and
    its tributaries, the hookline or each end of the net must be marked
    by a standard marker ball 50 cm in diameter or by a buoy with a
    shaft whose upper extremity is at least 92 cm above the water and
    has attached to it an orange flag measuring at least 22 cm by 22 cm.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 26
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-42-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 42"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Commercial Fishing Close Times]{.HTitleText2} {#h-735818 .Subheading}

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** No person who is fishing
under a commercial fishing licence or a commercial bait licence shall
fish for, or catch and retain, a species of fish set out in column 2 of
Schedule 7 or 8, as the case may be, in the waters set out in column 1
during the close time set out in column 3.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2014-311, s. 9
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-43-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 43"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 4]{.HLabel1}[Observers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-735822 .Part}

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  provincial Minister may designate a person who does not purchase fish
  for the purpose of resale and who is not an owner, operator, manager
  or employee of an enterprise that catches, cultures, processes or
  transports fish as an observer for the purposes of these Regulations
  and may assign to the observer any of the following duties:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the monitoring of fishing activities, the
    examination and measurement of fishing gear, the recording of
    scientific data and observations and the taking of samples;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the monitoring of the landing of fish and the
    verification of the weight and species of fish caught and retained;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the examination of records relating to fishing
    activities; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the biological examination and sampling of fish.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The provincial Minister shall provide each observer
  with a certificate of designation as an observer.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} An observer shall, on request, show the certificate
  of designation.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person who is operating a fishing vessel under a
  commercial fishing licence shall,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at the request of an observer, permit the observer
    to go on board the vessel to perform any of their duties; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provide the observer with such assistance as is
    reasonably necessary to enable the observer to perform those duties,
    including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} facilitating the sending and receiving of
      messages by means of the communications equipment on board the
      vessel,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} providing the position of the vessel in latitude
      and longitude using Loran C reading, Global Positioning System or
      any equivalent system,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} giving access to all areas of the vessel
      involved in fishing or fish processing or storage operations,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} providing suitable storage facilities for
      samples,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} permitting photographs or video recordings to be
      taken of fishing operations, gear and equipment,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} permitting embarkation and disembarkation at
      agreed times and places, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} permitting the removal from the vessel of
      samples, records, photographs and film taken or made on board the
      vessel.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2018-216, s. 27
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-44-20080101.html "Link to previous version of section 44"){.PITLink}
:::
